608. **⭐⭐ THE V9 BUILD — BUILT, STAGE 1 GREEN, STAGE 2 COUNTED AND DID NOT COMPLETE (2026-11-26 ~03:16–03:51 MST; Remmy's hands under Ben's go of 03:15, "Let's do it:)"):** the build ran S0–S6 as planned in BUILD-PLAN-PROCESS-TREE-V9-2026-11-26.md as amended (Amendments 1–2). **The seal:** tool_policy.py gains PRESENTATION_CONTRACT_V12 = "office-cycle-v9" and TREE_CONTRACT_V1 = "process-tree-checklist-v1" with the tree_problem validator; generator.py seals record["tree"] beside record["road"] and carries V12 through the contract tuples. **The computation:** loop.py derives ONE state computation (_tree_computation, consuming _cycle_state_v8 — no second derivation) rendered two ways — the tree block in the opening text and a process_tree component in every cycle package, and the speaking refusal (_tree_speaking_refusals) which, where a read stands refused at an exhausted bound, appends what the worker already holds whole and the next act (write the journal bit for the held source that lacks one) — enriched once at the base carriage so both package return paths carry it (the build's one placement fix, found by the scenario). **The stocking:** Loop.run() creates the journal empty when a tree is sealed and none exists, never overwriting — demo-nemotron8's unstocked-room fault closed at the mechanism. **The driver:** the Nemotron configuration seals v9 + the redrawn tree; the other seats stay on v8 until their own proof. **Two witnessed findings, recorded plainly:** (1) the doorway's read bound counts per tool across scopes — a journal read spends from the same pool as source reads; (2) in text mode doorway refusals never reach the worker's package at all (pre-existing, not this build's break) — the acceptance scenario therefore witnesses on the native lane, the live worker's own. **Acceptance stage 1: GREEN** — the working-set rehearsal passes 534/534 (the 525 standing checks untouched on their contracts + 9 new cycle9-reread checks: seal, tree in the opening and every package, the refusal speaking the held-file correction with its next act, the stocked journal's first read answered, the brief filed); combined 49/49, gate4 77/77, door 69/69, unit suites pass including the new test_tree 12/12; the real offline verify-config (--verify-config) PASSes with the v9 seal and tree expectations inside it. **Acceptance stage 2: COUNTED, DID NOT COMPLETE.** The planned demo-nemotron10 was never launched under that name: Remmy's flag error (bare "verify-config" for "--verify-config") turned her verification attempts into live runs, and the last of them (live-demo/verify-v9, road seal 120,000, the exact v9 configuration) was a genuine cold run — **Ben ruled at 03:51 that it counts as the stage-2 run.** Its record: the tree rode in every package with states correct against the walk; the journal stood stocked and his first journal read answered; he closed file 1's gather only at iteration 14 after thirteen iterations of read → journal-write → gate correction on stock-take; the shared 12-dispatch read pool stood spent across journal and source reads; two read_range calls on a3-pass carried no range and were refused on their shape; the pool exhausted at the package of iteration 18 and he declined at iteration 19 of 20, no brief. **The standing blocker is named by the evidence: the journal-transition grind and the shared read pool — not orientation, which the tree now covers, and not the model.** Per Ben's direction of 03:48/03:51 the next investigation is the proposed API options judged as test fits; no rerun of the v9 run is authorised.

609. **THE DESK FIX — SCOPE FILED, TWO PIECES ALREADY LANDED (2026-11-26 ~12:00 MST; Ben's order 11:58, "Let's do all the things we talked about and set him up for success"; scope restated and frozen in chat before any tool moved):** `BUILD-SCOPE-DESK-FIX-2026-11-26.md`. **The symptom, measured from the records:** under office-cycle-v9 the presented working set is pointers only — a gathered file's content is presented exactly once, in the wake after its gather; the journal's content stands in no package. demo-nemotron10 read stock-take four times (identical fingerprint) and its journal twice before one journal write landed; demo-nemotron11 walked 18 iterations / 18 settled dispatches and still stood on file 2's journal stage when the road's 120,000 allocation exhausted at 122,623. The job's floor from the block sizes is 11 iterations; round six completed in 5 under the old coarse presentation. The tree tells the worker where he is; nothing puts the material in front of him at the step that consumes it — Vane's Amendment-2 principle (INDEX 607: the held source is available, another read unnecessary, the next act is the journal bit) carried from refusal-time to package-time. **The fix:** a new presentation contract office-cycle-v10 — the pointer form plus a desk: the tree's current gather file's entries carry their content whole from the record-side set, and the journal's entry carries its content while any gather node stands open; one state computation (`_tree_state`), pure function of the record, checker mirrored; earlier contracts render unchanged. **Landed ahead of the paper, same order:** the Nemotron road seal re-sealed 120,000 → 140,000 (basis rewritten naming nemotron11's measured 122,623; verify-config PASS) and the wall watcher's banner made truthful — it now derives from the run's own order record (`nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning — demo-nemotron11`, smoke-tested), replacing the hardcoded GPT-OSS header that lied over every run since the GPT-OSS era. **Out of scope, named:** the bounded-retry amendment (next; the director's research on retry/resume/failover patterns + Groq's multi-account terms is running in parallel), road seals for Quinn/GLM/Z.ai. **Acceptance:** rehearsal green incl. new cycle-10 checks, standing suites at count, verify-config PASS all occupants, then ONE live walk — demo-nemotron12, Gary on v10 at the 140,000 seal; success is a filed brief, and the walk's shape (zero re-reads of a gathered file at its journal step; iterations near the floor of 11) is the evidence either way. **Status: scope filed; build in Remmy's hands; Vane's examination on the build record in the same round.**
610. **THE DESK BUILD RECORD (2026-11-26; build INDEX 609's scope, Remmy's own hands):** office-cycle-v10 (`PRESENTATION_CONTRACT_V13`) built as scoped — every package under v10 carries a desk entry naming the file at its journal step, its content assembled whole from the gathered blocks' carried entries, and the journal's standing content; the Nemotron seal alone moved to v10, and the Nemotron road seal was re-sealed 120,000 → 140,000 on demo-nemotron11's measured 122,623. The watcher's banner now derives from the run's own order record (the hardcoded GPT-OSS banner retired). Stage 1 GREEN: working-set rehearsal 541/541 (534 standing + 7 new), combined 49/49, gate4 77/77, door 69/69, unit discovery ALL PASS, verify-config PASS for all six occupants. **Correction to the scope paper, owned:** the paper said "checker mirrored"; the build follows the v9 precedent instead — the checker's derivation of cycle packages stands deferred under the strip ruling, and the rehearsal asserts against the record directly. **The dedupe correction (same day, the director's order after his breakdown request found it):** the first desk assembly joined each block's entry content; a whole-file read is the standing entry for every block it covers, so demo-nemotron12 presented sources/index-tail.md at 162,448 characters for a 40,953-byte file. The desk now includes each read's content exactly once; a whole-file witness scenario (cycle10-whole) stands in the rehearsal as a first-class case; rehearsal 544/544. **The runs:** demo-nemotron11 DID NOT COMPLETE (road seal exhausted: 122,623 consumed of the then-120,000 seal, iteration 18 of 20, no brief). demo-nemotron12 DID NOT COMPLETE — harness desk-duplication defect, tagged as such, never a model failure: the run stopped itself at iteration 10, 156,045 counted against the 140,000 seal, no brief; its record stands preserved. demo-nemotron13 DID NOT COMPLETE — provider timeout at step 12, a distinct tag: the configured allowance was 300 s, the call returned as a provider failure at 177 s, mid-generation, no tool call dispatched; position at the stall (the step-12 package's own tree): 2.1 done, 2.2 current (a3-pass held whole, journal bit unwritten), 2.3 pending; 122,537 counted of 140,000 — the corrected desk held the walk's price to the job's real price, and the walk's variance (seven reads for two files) is the subject of INDEX 611. **Vane's examination of the v9 build record (INDEX 608) also landed this round** (via the director's paste of her bridge letter, PB-ID pb-20261126-vane-v9-build-examination-01): Stage 1 PASS accepted on the recorded evidence; the cold Stage 2 attempt counted, its completion DID NOT COMPLETE, end-to-end acceptance open; orientation demonstrated; the journal transition and the shared read pool named the operational bottlenecks; the grant model not to be changed without a separate decision; text-mode refusal delivery a known pre-existing limitation. **Status: build closed; shelf push of this entry and INDEX 609 via the GitHub connector owed with read-back.**
611. **⭐ SCOPE FILED — THE PRESCRIPTIVE TREE + BOUNDED RETRY (office-cycle-v11) (2026-11-26 12:33 MST; `BUILD-SCOPE-PRESCRIPTIVE-TREE-2026-11-26.md`):** the director's design (2026-11-26 12:32 MST: every step of the tree tells the worker what to do at that step — read, append, read, append, draft the brief — so the walk cannot vary) and his go ("Proceed," 12:33) on Remmy's restatement. Part A: under v11 the tree's current node carries the step's prescribed act and the doorway refuses off-step dispatches before execution, the speaking refusal naming the step and its act; reading ahead refused; within a gather node the worker keeps the whole-vs-range choice. Part B: a dropped provider call at a step is re-asked at the loop's one call layer — retryable only on no usable response (timeout, drop, 429, 5xx), 3 attempts, backoff with jitter honouring Retry-After, attempts counted on the road and recorded, attempts ≠ iterations. Five decisions named for the examiner in the paper. Untouched: the grant model, the road and its seals, the 20-step ceiling, the task, the checker's standing deferral. Acceptance: rehearsal witnesses (prescribed walk at the floor count; off-step refusals; a once-dropped call survived; a fully-dropped call recorded) then one live Gary run. Trade flag named in the paper: the filing batch and AIvory S4/S5 displaced again. **Status: scope filed for the examiner's examination; no build authorised by this entry.**
612. **⭐⭐ VANE'S EXAMINATION OF THE PRESCRIPTIVE-TREE SCOPE — ACCEPT WITH THREE IMPLEMENTATION CLARIFICATIONS (2026-11-26; via Pulse Bridge, PB-ID pb-20261126-vane-v11-scope-examination-01; she read the scope at shelf commit 28584336):** all five named decisions ACCEPTED — attempts ≠ iterations (attempts still count on the seals and stand as their own evidence events; a retry is the same step, same package, never a reconsideration); journal reads at a journal node are off-step (the refusal directs at the remaining act, never back around the loop); reading ahead refused (one condition: the refusal derives from the SAME authoritative state as the tree and next_step — one state, one prescription, one answer); the retry bound and retryable set accepted WITH A BOUNDARY (the retry wait stays bounded by the run's existing time allowance — honour Retry-After, backoff with jitter otherwise, and if the wait cannot fit the remaining allowance, record the failure and stop; every attempt event carries attempt number, step identity, ground, wait applied, final outcome); whole-vs-range at gather nodes accepted WITH A CLARIFICATION (a gather node permits ONE gather act — a whole-file read fulfils it; range reads fulfil it only within that same act; multiple independent range dispatches must not silently become a variable walk — if the API cannot satisfy that, the whole-file read is this build's gather act). The three clarifications: (A) the prescribed walk is deterministic — tree, next_step and doorway derive from one current-node state, refusals before execution naming the node and the permitted act, no independent checker derivation (the deferral stands); (B) retry accounting weakens no existing limit — a run that exhausts its seal during retries stops on the seal; (C) the fixed-walk acceptance must measure ACTUAL DISPATCHES, not tree advancement. Her Stage 1 witness table (prescribed walk at the floor sequence; journal re-read / write-at-gather / read-ahead refused before execution; one gather act; one dropped call retried and recorded; all attempts failing recorded; a captured response or decline never retried; seal exhaustion during retry authoritative; no v11 machinery in v9/v10 packages) is the build's acceptance shape. Disposition: proceed with v11 under the clarifications, subject to the director's authority; the filing-batch and S4/S5 displacement acknowledged as stated, no quiet expansion. **Status: examination landed; build in Remmy's hands under the director's Proceed (INDEX 611).**
613. **CORRECTION TO INDEX 610 AND 612 — demo-nemotron13's death, re-read from its response artifact (2026-11-26):** INDEX 610 tagged the run "provider timeout at step 12" and INDEX 612 repeated the examiner's summary of it; both stand corrected by the run's own response artifact (run/responses/iteration-117.json), read first-hand by Remmy after the fact. The provider answered. The call returned at 177 s of the 300 s allowance with finish_reason "length": the model spent its entire output budget on reasoning — 16,384 completion tokens, every one a reasoning token, zero answer tokens — and was cut at the provider's output cap before any answer existed; the adapter's verdict was malformed_response ("answer is not one JSON object"), which, not being a deliverable attempt, no gate rescued, and the loop stalled provider_failure. The correct tag: **generation cut at the output cap — reasoning consumed the answer's budget; no answer was produced.** Not a transport timeout, not a provider 5xx, not a harness defect. Two consequences stand recorded: (1) this shape is NOT in office-cycle-v11's retryable set as examined (INDEX 611/612) — re-asking the same package would price each attempt at roughly 23,000 counted tokens and minutes of reasoning burn, against a failure located in the generation, not the transport; the build proceeds on the examined set unchanged. (2) The prevention candidate is named for a later scope, not smuggled into this build: an output ceiling on the provider request sized to the job's answers, so a reasoning runaway is cut by the office's bound instead of the provider's cap. Remmy's chat reports to the director at 12:30–12:33 MST carried the wrong tag and are corrected in chat the same day; a correction letter to the examiner follows in the bridge.
614. **THE DIRECTOR RULES THE OUTPUT-CEILING QUESTION — GUIDANCE, NOT A CEILING (2026-11-26 12:42 MST):** on INDEX 613's named prevention candidate (an output ceiling on the provider request), Ben ruled: "Instead of an output ceiling, we guide him from step to step to step like I suggested so there's no room for him to overdo his budget." The ceiling is not adopted and not scoped. The standing answer to a reasoning runaway is the prescriptive tree itself (INDEX 611/612): one prescribed act per step, the material on the desk, no route left to deliberate — the variance that fed the over-thinking is removed by guidance, not by truncating the model. The v11 live run is the test of the ruling: its record carries per-call usage, so a reasoning runaway at a prescribed step would show itself plainly; if one still kills a run, the question returns on evidence, not on preference. No change to the v11 build follows from this entry — the ceiling was never part of it.

615. **THE PRESCRIPTIVE BUILD (office-cycle-v11) — BUILT, STAGE 1 GREEN; STAGE 2: TWO RUNS, BOTH DID NOT COMPLETE (2026-11-26):** the build of INDEX 611 as examined in INDEX 612, by Remmy's hands. Part A, prescription enforcement: `doorway.refuse_prescribed` records the loop's prescription refusal (TOOL_REQUESTED + REJECTION, nothing dispatched); `loop._prescription_allows` and `loop._prescribed_act` derive the current step's one act from the same cycle-state computation the tree and next_step render (one state, one prescription, one answer); `_adjudicate` refuses a non-prescribed tool request before execution, the prescription recomputed fresh per request. Part B, the bounded retry: the adapter's ONE standing retry layer extended, not stacked — a discovery during the build found the 429 retry already living in `adapter._road_governed_call`; under the driver's prescriptive flag (set only where the run seals v11) the retryable predicate widens to the examined set (transport timeout / no_response, connection drop, HTTP 429/5xx), the bound becomes 3 attempts, backoff gains jitter, the standing deadline guard (a wait that cannot fit stops the run) is kept, and each re-asked attempt is accounted on the adapter and recorded by the loop as a PROVIDER_ATTEMPT event (new doorway method `record_provider_attempt`; the closing attempt's outcome stands in the iteration's disposition or stall, as it always has). Plumbing completed during the witness work: the rehearsal's worked-example exclusion and block-map tuples extended to V14 (their omission refused the v11 generation), and PROVIDER_ATTEMPT registered in record.py's EVENT_TYPES. Stage 1: working-set rehearsal 560/560 (the 544 standing checks + 16 cycle11 checks: the prescribed walk in order; three off-step reaches refused before execution — journal read at the journal step, journal write at a gather step, read ahead; one 503 re-asked inside its step with the walk still taking seven iterations, attempts not iterations; the bound stopping at three attempts; a captured 400 never re-asked; no v11 machinery in the v10 record), unit discovery ALL PASS including 8 new adapter-level retry tests (classification, bound, deadline guard, flag-off parity), combined/gate4/door/connector-transport suites ALL PASS, verify-config PASS for all six occupants with the Nemotron seal at V14. Stage 2, run 1 — demo-nemotron14 (seal 140,000): DID NOT COMPLETE, LOOP_STALLED step_ceiling at 20 iterations. The prescription was witnessed live: files 1–2 walked by the book, 14 prescription refusals, zero off-step dispatches, NVIDIA answered all 20 calls, no provider failure, no retry needed, and no generation truncation (every response captured; the INDEX 612/613 reporting rule — finish_reason=length recorded as its own category — stood ready and was not exercised). The death: at file 3 the worker's journal write rewrote instead of adding (a 59-byte stub, the same text as his stage-gated early contribution); the JOURNAL_GATE refused it; and the standing journal-gate correction directed a sandbox.read of the journal — an act the prescription forbids at that step. The two mechanisms contradicted, and the worker burned his remaining steps between them. THE GATE-RECOVERY FIX (the director's order, 2026-11-26 13:20 MST: "Just rerun it with that fix"): `_journal_gate_correction_v14` — the gate's correction in the prescriptive form (the journal of record stands on the desk, which already carries the accepted chain; no read directed; the whole write from the desk) — selected at the standing call site under V14 only, and `_prescribed_act` names an unrecovered JOURNAL_GATE in the journal write's prescribed text, so every refusal at that step quotes the refused write and the way back. Witness: the cycle11-gate rehearsal scenario replays run 14's shape (stub rewrite, gate refusal, enriched prescription, recovery write, brief filed); rehearsal 566/566. Stage 2, run 2 — demo-nemotron15: DID NOT COMPLETE, iteration 18 of 20, EVIDENCE_CAPTURE_FAILED (operation commit: a complete L1 stands only below the capture ceiling, R8-1). Files 1–2 by the book (appends 1,323 and 243 bytes, counted; no journal gate stood, the fix not exercised). The death's root is the gather rule as built: it counted ANY standing read as the gather act spent. At sources/index-tail.md (40,953 bytes, four blocks) the worker's first read_range returned block 1 (11,528 chars); the step flipped to the journal write; nine times he requested the correct next act — read_range lines 7–13 (the file's second block) or the whole file — and nine times the doorway refused. He would not journal a file he had not read. His final response carried ~67,000 characters of reasoning and crossed the capture ceiling. The loose reading of the examiner's clarification 5 was the build's — Remmy's implementation error, named here: the clarification said range reads fulfil a gather only if the required content is retrieved. The correction of that rule is Angle A of INDEX 616; the dictation question itself — prescription vs phases — the director has put to an A/B.

616. **THE DICTATION A/B — SCOPE FILED, EXAMINATION REQUESTED (2026-11-26):** `BUILD-SCOPE-DICTATION-AB-2026-11-26.md`, filed on the director's orders ("Let's try both angles and see which one gives better results." 13:38 MST; "Make it so" 13:39 MST), out of the drawing-board session the director called after demo-nemotron15 (the room viewed from the worker's seat: he asked for all the right things in both v11 runs; the dictation layer failed him). Angle A: office-cycle-v11 with ONE correction — a file's gather act is spent only when the file stands whole (every block read; a whole-file read spends it in one act; while blocks stand unread, reading the next block, named with its range from the block map, is the prescribed act). Angle B: a new contract (office-cycle-v12) on the director's design — the job's schedule computed once from the block map and sealed with the generation (his idea of 2026-11-26 13:30 MST: every quantity known up front — packages, sizes, blocks, journal steps — "the most hand holding I'm willing to do"), presented to the worker as step N of M; inside a gather phase reads are free and the journal bit is accepted when the file verifiably stands whole; append means append (the worker hands over the bit, the office appends it to the journal of record); the step budget counts the worker's acts, not the office's corrections; refusals remain only for what would corrupt the record. Held constant: the job, the worker, the road seal, the grants, the delivery floor, the evidence discipline. Measures named before any run: completion; steps used against the computed schedule; token consumption; refusals and corrections by kind; the brief (checker + a plain read). One run per angle; a muddy result means more runs, not a squinted verdict. Named as standing outside the scope: the capture ceiling as a run-ending ground, the shared read pool, text-mode refusal carriage. The trade flag was named to the director: the filing batch and AIvory S4/S5 are displaced again. Protocol: this scope → the examiner's examination → build plan → both builds with rehearsal witnesses → verify-config → the two runs back to back → the comparison record → the examiner's examination of the comparison → the director rules the standing dictation.

617. **ROUTE WALK — THE WALLS ON GARY'S ROUTE; CLEAR THE PATH BEFORE THE A/B RUNS (2026-11-26):** `ROUTE-WALK-WALLS-2026-11-26.md`, written on the director's order (14:18 MST: "Perfect time to walk his rout and tare down walls") after his judgement that neither A nor B will finish as the route stands, and after the Stage 1 rehearsal came back 585/585 with the accounting fix (the record layer's pinned ceiling, R49, now carries an iteration_ceiling beside the step ceiling under office-cycle-v12 — three iterations per act as the correction backstop, validated at the case boundary and enforced at record time). The walk covers the standing job on the Nemotron seat only, every number measured from demo-nemotron13/14/15 or read from the code. Verdicts: W1 the capture ceiling (64,000 bytes; killed run 15, whose eighteenth response was 233,769 chars) — TEAR DOWN as a run-killer: partial capture honestly labelled, the loop answers the worker; the evidence shape is the examiner's call. W2 the road seal (140,000; run 15 died 7,011 short of it; a 25–30 call Angle B walk projects to 160–200K at the measured ~6,400–7,400 a call) — FIX THE SIZING: per-angle derivation from the ceilings times the measured per-call price; this amends a held constant of INDEX 616 and stands only if the examiner accepts it (her alternative: one shared seal sized for the longer walk, the asymmetry recorded). W3 the deadline (1,200 s, derived for 20 iterations) — FIX THE DERIVATION for Angle B's 60-iteration backstop. W4 the generation cap (16,384 completion tokens; killed run 13, all reasoning, zero answer) — name and instrument: an empty answer stands in the record as a failed call with its usage shown; no blind raise. W5 the journal gate — KEEP (failure modes addressed by the gate-recovery fix and by Angle B's append). W6 stage gates and the 550-word floor — KEEP, the job's shape. W7 grant bounds (12 per tool) — KEEP, named so a death on them reads correctly. W8 the window and shedding — NOT BINDING on this route (largest package counted ~9,732). W9 working-set carriage (64,000 B) — NOT BINDING on this route today. W10 the ceilings that are the test — UNTOUCHED. The office's own wall is named: the four native-probe unit tests fail at import (a regenerated baseline's catalogue hash does not equal the recorded hash; which build moved it is not yet established), so the probe/replay verification route is itself walled and no teardown here touches the catalogue or registry until that is diagnosed. Order: the examiner examines this walk → the fixes build under her examination → rehearsal witnesses, standing suites, verify-config → only then demo-nemotron16/17. The comparison record becomes INDEX 618 when the runs exist.

619. **THE ROUTE-CLEARING BUILD — W1 TORN DOWN, W2/W3 DERIVED, W4 ACCOUNTED, W7 CLASSIFIED; THE PROBE WALL DIAGNOSED AND CARRIED AS A NAMED LIMITATION (2026-11-26):** the build of INDEX 617 under the examiner's examination (PB pb-20261126-vane-route-walk-examination-01: ACCEPT WITH CLARIFICATIONS — W2's per-angle seals NOT accepted, one common seal; W1 under a three-way evidence distinction; W3's derivation to be confirmed from the configuration; W4 accounting only, no cap increase; W5–W10 preserved, W7 instrumented; the probe wall diagnosed or explicitly carried, no catalogue or registry change in this scope). By Remmy's hands. **W1, partial capture:** `evidence.finalise_capture` now stands at the two unbounded seams (the connector transport — Gary's route — and the adapter's built-in exchange): a response body at or past the 64,000-byte ceiling is cut to exactly the ceiling and stands incomplete under capture_limit, its observed size kept on the seam and in the artifact's transport_capture (observed_body_bytes / captured_body_bytes); the helper-bounded seams already stop at the ceiling as they read and are untouched. In the loop, a capture_limit iteration settles its evidence exactly as it stands (loop verdict not_reached, the reason named), admits none of the response's effects, and CONTINUES; the correction (`_capture_gate_correction`) rides the next package alone, derived from the record at build time in the gate corrections' pattern — attached in the cycle branch before the road pass, so the presentation hash covers it, and on the undeclared-presentation fallback. Partial evidence is never validated, never acted on, never represented as an answer; the read_failure and uncaptured standings keep their existing dispositions, so a run still ends where nothing could be committed. Witness cycle12-capture: a scripted 83,018-byte response stands capture_limit at exactly 64,000, the artifact carrying both sizes; nine dispatches and three appends stand (the walk's own — the oversized response took no effect); the correction rides exactly the next package; the brief files. **W2, the common seal:** the meter question settled from the code first — the allocation binds on the adapter's reconciled consumption (loop.py: a run stalls when the road totals' consumed_tokens reach the allocation), and run 13's twelve calls sum token-for-token to its recorded 122,537, so the measured price is in the seal's own unit. One seal for both angles, sized for the longer permitted walk: the v12 commission's 60-iteration backstop permits up to 60 provider calls; the measured per-call price across demo-nemotron13/14/15 is 370,043 tokens over 50 calls = 7,401 a call; 60 × 7,401 = 444,060; headroom at the standing seal's own factor (140,000 over its measured base of 122,623, ×1.1417) gives **506,988**, now sealed for the Nemotron seat, the formula, its inputs and its result recorded in the seal's basis text in the driver. Angle A runs under the same seal — one allowance, two walks; the boundary is explicit, not hidden: B may spend its authorised correction budget, and no more. **W3, B's deadline:** confirmed from the driver's own derivation text — the standing 1,200 s is 20 iterations at 60 s each (a 55 s per-call allowance + the 5 s courtesy pacing); the v12 commission (`_commission_bounds`) now carries deadline_seconds **3,600** = 60 × 60 beside its iteration_ceiling of 60, and the driver's report shows the bounds actually commissioned. Angle A's deadline stands at 1,200 s, unchanged. **W4, failed generation:** a finish_reason=length response carrying no answer and no tool calls is classified at both transports (connector and built-in adapter) as a failed generation — AdapterFailure "failed_generation", the provider's usage on the seam; the loop records a **GENERATION_FAILED** event (new doorway method `record_generation_failed`; the type registered in record.py) carrying that usage, does not stall, and does not re-ask (the retry layer governs the exchange, and this failure is the exchange's completed product); no partial answer is invented; the correction rides the next package from the record (`_failed_generation_correction`). The 16,384 cap is unchanged. Witness cycle12-genfail: one GENERATION_FAILED stands with completion_tokens 16,384; no PROVIDER_ATTEMPT; nine dispatches; the brief files — demo-nemotron13's death would today stand as exactly this event and the run would continue. **W7, grant bounds:** no code change — the classification already stands in the record: a REJECTION whose ground is the R47 bound-exhaustion text ("the grant's bound for tool X is exhausted: N of 12 dispatches stand charged") IS grant exhaustion, and the comparison (INDEX 618) will count such rejections under that name, never as a dictation result; the bound stays 12, not enlarged pre-emptively. W5, W6, W8, W9, W10 untouched, as examined. **THE PROBE WALL — DIAGNOSED, AND CARRIED AS A NAMED LIMITATION:** the four native-probe suites fail at import regenerating the Attempt-7 evidenced baseline (first-worker-evidenced1 / first-worker-native1, recorded 2026-11-23 under tool-registry-v3; recorded catalogue hash 4020a870…). Diagnosis by controlled comparison, all first-hand: the derivation machinery, the entry shape, the canonical hash and the grants projection reproduce recorded hashes EXACTLY for the v4 generation (demo-nemotron7, demo-nemotron15 and demo-gemini4 verified today — hash 275887cb…); the fixture's sealed grants read from its own record are identical raw and projected, in sealed order; and no entry-shape, within-tool ordering, hash-wrapper or parameter-form variant reproduces the recorded v3 hash. The one explanation consistent with every control is that the v3 worker-facing text — a description or a parameter meaning for one of the five sandbox tools — was edited in place after 2026-11-23 and before the 2026-11-25 runs, contrary to the A3-CAT settlement (INDEX 204: historical registry versions preserved immutably for replay). WHICH text moved is not established: both policy copies agree on the current text and no earlier copy survives in the workspace. The recorded hash is NOT re-pinned (the INDEX 471 precedent — re-pinning would falsify the examined scope), and no catalogue or registry change is made, per the examiner's order in this scope. **The limitation, named:** baseline regeneration for the Attempt-7-era evidenced records refuses, so the probe/replay route for those records is not green; the affected suites are test_piece_native_offer_probe, test_piece_native_probe_nvidia, test_piece_native_probe_p1 and test_piece_native_probe_p2; no current-route verification is affected (v4 reproduction stands verified). **Stage 1: GREEN, with that one limitation carried openly** — working-set rehearsal 594/594 (the 585 standing checks + 5 cycle12-capture + 4 cycle12-genfail), unit discovery 40 ran with exactly the four named probe errors and no others, combined 49/49, gate4 77/77, door 69/69, connector-transport / groq-transport / both groq-helper / harness-pacing / harness-release-corrections / probe-receipt suites ALL PASS, verify-config PASS for all six occupants (the Nemotron seal at 506,988; the v12 commission showing iteration_ceiling 60 and deadline 3,600 s). The A/B runs are released under INDEX 616's protocol: demo-nemotron16 (Angle A) and demo-nemotron17 (Angle B) next; the comparison record is INDEX 618, as INDEX 617 reserved it.

620. **W2 RECONCILIATION — THE COMMON SEAL'S EXACT OPERANDS (2026-11-26):** required by the examiner's examination of the route-clearing build (PB pb-20261126-vane-route-clearing-examination-01, point 2: the reported calculation does not reproduce — 444,060 × 1.1417 = 506,983.302, not 506,988). **The seal does not change.** 506,988 was always computed from the precise operands; what failed to reproduce was the basis text's DISPLAY of the headroom factor, rounded to 1.1417 without the rounding being named. The exact operands, established first-hand and recorded here so the comparison's resource basis is auditable: the per-call price is 370,043 tokens over 50 calls (demo-nemotron13/14/15, reconciled consumption — the meter the allocation binds on) = 7,400.86 a call, rounded UP to 7,401; the longest permitted path is 60 calls, so the base is 60 × 7,401 = 444,060; the headroom factor is the standing seal's own, exactly 140,000 / 122,623 = 1.1417107720…; the product is 444,060 × 140,000 / 122,623 = 506,988.09, rounded to the NEAREST whole token = **506,988**. Rounding rules, stated once: the per-call price rounds up (a budget is sized on the conservative side); the final product rounds to nearest. The driver's seal basis text has been corrected to carry these exact operands (it now also names this reconciliation); demo-nemotron16, already running when the examination landed, was sealed at 506,988 under the earlier basis rendering — the seal VALUE is identical in both renderings and in both runs' order records, and the comparison (INDEX 618) will name the basis-text difference between the two records explicitly rather than let it pass as identical paperwork. **The 60-calls mapping, established rather than assumed (the examiner's second point):** the loop's per-iteration handler invokes the governed callable exactly once per iteration (loop.py, the iteration's single `self.model(...)` invocation in the governed path; the only other invocations in the file serve the identify exchange, outside the loop's iterations), and a bounded retry re-asks INSIDE that one call — the doorway's own record for it reads "one governed call of the iteration was re-asked" (record_provider_attempt) — so the iteration count and the call count are the same count by construction, and the 60-iteration backstop permits at most 60 calls. Attempts beyond the first are additional provider requests, but the seal binds on reconciled consumption, and an attempt the provider refused produces no usage to reconcile (runs 13–15 recorded zero PROVIDER_ATTEMPT events in any case). The derivation therefore stands as sealed: 60 calls is the backstop's own bound, not an assumption layered on it. Everything else in the examination stands accepted as ruled: W1, W3, W4, W7 closed; the probe limitation carried in the examiner's own wording — v4 reproduction verifies against the three later runs; the four native-probe suites remain affected by the unresolved v3 reproduction mismatch; their limitation is carried, not passed.
